</details>


### [90] [Predicting plasmonic pole representations of complex dielectric spectra using convolutional neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11698)
*Dario A. Leon*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种卷积神经网络框架，通过多极-帕德（multipole-Padé）表示，将介电谱直接反演为等离子激元极点结构，实现高通量、自动化的介电谱分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从介电谱中提取能量位置、展宽和谱权等谱学性质对解释多体物理中的集体电子激发至关重要；但传统方法依赖非线性拟合，计算量大且对初始化和拟合选择高度敏感。

Method: 开发卷积神经网络，采用多极-帕德解析表达式随机生成参数化的合成谱作为训练数据，使网络无偏地学习光谱与特征之间的一般映射，而无需依赖特定材料的大规模数据集。

Result: 合成训练的网络能泛化到复杂的第一性原理和实验介电谱，在前向单次传递中高精度地提取底层极点参数。

Conclusion: 该方法为传统非线性拟合提供了高效、鲁棒的替代方案，可实现对多种材料和应用场景介电谱的高通量自动化分析。

Abstract: Extracting spectral properties such as energy position, broadening, and spectral weight from dielectric spectra is critical for interpreting collective electronic excitations in many-body physics. Conventional approaches typically rely on nonlinear fitting procedures that can be computationally demanding and highly sensitive to initialization and fitting choices. In this work, we develop a convolutional neural-network framework for the direct inversion of dielectric spectra into their underlying plasmonic pole structures using a multipole-Padé representation. Rather than training on a constrained database of spectra associated with a specific set of materials, the network is trained entirely on synthetic spectra generated from the analytical multipole-Padé expression with randomized parameters. This enables the network to learn the general mapping between the spectra and their features in an unbiased way, without requiring large, material-specific datasets derived from real materials. We demonstrate that the synthetically trained network generalizes to complex first-principles and experimental dielectric spectra, extracting the underlying pole parameters with high accuracy in a single forward pass. This approach provides an efficient and robust alternative to conventional nonlinear fitting, enabling high-throughput, automated analysis of dielectric spectra across diverse materials and applications.

</details>


### [91] [Interlayer ferromagnetic order revealed through pressure-enhanced interlayer exchange coupling in Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11731)
*Riku Iimori,Takashi Kimura*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 作者对高质量Fe3GaTe2单晶进行静水压磁化测量，发现居里温度以下的Tinf1和Tinf2两个特征温度具有相反的压强依赖性，分别对应热激活畴壁脱钉和层间铁磁序的形成；第一性原理计算证明压强显著增强层间交换作用，从而可由微观层间交换耦合预测层间磁序的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德瓦尔斯铁磁体的层间磁耦合对其磁性起关键作用，但如何在压强等外部调控下从微观交换耦合出发理解并预测层间磁序的形成，仍缺乏明确的定量框架。

Method: 在Fe3GaTe2高质量单晶上进行静水压下的磁化测量，选择性调控层间耦合；识别居里温度以下的两个特征温度Tinf1、Tinf2并分析其压强依赖；辅以第一性原理计算层间交换相互作用随压强的变化并与实验对照。

Result: 发现Tinf1对应热激活畴壁脱钉、Tinf2对应层间铁磁序形成，二者压强依赖相反且均显著，而居里温度（对应层内铁磁序）几乎不随压强变化；第一性原理计算显示压强大幅增强层间交换作用，并能合理重现Tinf2及其压强依赖。

Conclusion: 层间磁序的出现可由微观层间交换耦合预测，建立了理解和设计层状磁性材料中层间耦合现象的预测框架。

Abstract: Van der Waals ferromagnets possess finite interlayer magnetic coupling despite their layered crystal structures, and this coupling plays an important role in determining their magnetic properties. In this study, we performed magnetization measurements on high-quality Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ single crystals while selectively tuning the interlayer coupling using hydrostatic pressure. We identified two characteristic temperatures, $T_{\rm inf1}$ and $T_{\rm inf2}$, below the Curie temperature with opposite pressure dependences: $T_{\rm inf1}$ is associated with thermally activated domain-wall depinning, whereas $T_{\rm inf2}$ reflects the formation of interlayer ferromagnetic order. Both temperatures exhibit pronounced pressure dependences, in sharp contrast to the nearly pressure-independent Curie temperature associated with intralayer ferromagnetic order. First-principles calculations revealed a substantial pressure-induced enhancement of the interlayer exchange interaction and reasonably reproduced $T_{\rm inf2}$ and its pressure dependence. These results demonstrate that the emergence of interlayer magnetic ordering can be predicted from the microscopic interlayer exchange coupling, establishing a predictive framework for understanding and designing interlayer coupling phenomena in layered magnetic materials.

</details>


### [92] [Can end-to-end learning from raw electronic structure explain magnetic anisotropy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11742)
*Rafał Topolnicki,Jan Navrátil,Piotr Błoński*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 机器学习模型可直接从电子能谱预测自旋-轨道驱动的磁各向异性能（MAE），但预测精度相近的模型可能学到不同的电子依赖性。本文比较双向GRU与一维CNN，发现其学到的谱依赖仅部分重叠，并通过SHAP、微扰理论（PT2）特征、谱扰动及域外迁移验证了物理相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测精度相当的机器学习模型可能学到不同的电子结构依赖关系，因此仅凭域内精度无法判断所学关系的物理意义，需要系统评估模型从电子能谱中学到的物理相关性。

Result: 两架构域内精度相当，但学到的谱依赖仅部分重叠；SHAP识别出的共享特征与PT2一致的自旋-轨道耦合路径吻合；加入PT2贡献对域内性能影响很小，但可改善域外迁移且增益因架构而异；受控谱扰动表明相似的归因模式不代表MAE对谱权重有相同的函数依赖，从而为架构相关的OOD迁移差异提供了解释。

Conclusion: 端到端学习可识别磁各向异性的候选电子特征，但其可信的微观解释需要来自预测性能、模型比较、物理理论、受控谱干预及分布偏移下评估的多方收敛证据支撑。

Abstract: Machine-learning models trained directly on electronic spectra can predict spin--orbit-driven properties, yet models with comparable predictive accuracy may learn different electronic dependencies. We examine this problem using magnetic anisotropy in atomic-scale magnets and a validation strategy designed to assess the physical relevance of learned spectral relationships. We compare a bidirectional gated recurrent unit and a one-dimensional convolutional neural network, both trained to predict magnetic anisotropy energy (MAE) from spin- and orbital-resolved scalar-relativistic densities of states (SR-DOS). Despite comparable in-domain (ID) accuracy, the architectures learn only partly overlapping spectral dependencies. Shapley additive explanations identify spectral features shared between the two architectures that can be associated with plausible spin--orbit-coupling pathways consistent with second-order perturbation theory (PT2). Adding PT2-derived MAE contributions to the model inputs has little effect on ID performance but can improve transfer beyond the training domain, with gains differing between architectures. Controlled spectral perturbations further demonstrate that similar attribution patterns do not imply the same functional dependence of the predicted MAE on spectral weight. The contrasting responses provide a functional context for architecture-dependent out-of-domain transfer. End-to-end learning can identify candidate electronic signatures of magnetic anisotropy. Their credible microscopic interpretation, however, rests on convergent evidence from predictive performance, model comparison, physical theory, controlled spectral interventions, and evaluation under distribution shift.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁性具有实空间补偿共线磁矩与偶宇称动量依赖自旋劈裂的特征，但在全补偿共线磁体中实现奇宇称和混合宇称磁性仍具挑战。作者旨在探索一种可调途径，突破传统铁磁/反铁磁范式，实现奇、混合宇称的自旋劈裂。

Method: 以单层 CoS 和 CoSe 为代表体系，结合 Floquet 理论与第一性原理计算，研究不同偏振光照射对共线反铁磁体的影响。采用圆偏振光（CPL）诱导奇宇称 f 波自旋劈裂，椭圆偏振光（EPL）诱导奇宇称 p 波自旋劈裂；进一步引入单轴应变配合 CPL 实现 p 波态，并通过外加电场与 CPL/EPL 结合将自旋劈裂转变为混合宇称形式，同时分析 PT 对称性破缺与自旋轨道耦合对反常霍尔响应的贡献。

Result: CPL 诱导奇宇称 f 波自旋劈裂，EPL 诱导奇宇称 p 波自旋劈裂；单轴应变结合 CPL 可作为实现 p 波态的替代途径；电场与 CPL 或 EPL 共同作用可将自旋劈裂转变为混合宇称形式；光诱导的 PT 对称性破缺与自旋轨道耦合共同导致有限的反常霍尔响应。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种在补偿反铁磁体中工程化奇宇称和混合宇称自旋劈裂的通用策略，为实现光控电子和磁现象提供了新途径，拓展了磁性序调控的物理机制与应用前景。

Abstract: Altermagnetism, characterized by compensated collinear magnetic moments in real space and momentum-dependent spin splitting with even-parity symmetry, has recently emerged as a distinct form of magnetic order beyond the conventional ferro- and antiferromagnetic paradigms. However, realizing odd- and mixed-parity magnetism in fully compensated collinear magnets remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate that light irradiation can induce odd- and mixed-parity magnetism in conventional collinear antiferromagnets. Taking monolayer CoS and CoSe as representative systems, and combining Floquet theory with first-principles calculations, we show that circularly polarized light (CPL) induces odd-parity $f$-wave spin splitting, whereas elliptically polarized light (EPL) induces odd-parity $p$-wave spin splitting. Uniaxial strain combined with CPL provides an alternative route to realizing the $p$-wave state. More remarkably, applying an electric field together with CPL or EPL transforms the spin splitting into mixed-parity forms. The light-induced breaking of $\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry, together with spin-orbit coupling, further gives rise to a finite anomalous Hall response. Our work establishes a versatile strategy for engineering odd- and mixed-parity spin splitting in compensated antiferromagnets, offering a route toward light-controlled electronic and magnetic phenomena.

</details>


### [94] [Solution-Based Synthesis of Fe-Co-Ni Prussian Blue Analogue Powders: A Comparative Structural, Spectroscopic and Thermal Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11830)

TL;DR: 本文通过无添加剂水相共沉淀法合成了Fe、Co、Ni三种普鲁士蓝类似物（PBA），并系统比较其结构、谱学、形貌与热学性质，发现三者差异不仅源于过渡金属种类，还与金属前驱体氧化态和洗涤效率密切相关。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普鲁士蓝及其类似物（PBAs）因开放框架结构在储能、催化等领域备受关注，但不同过渡金属取代会显著影响其组成与性能。作者希望通过统一合成条件，系统厘清Fe、Co、Ni三种PBA之间的性质差异，并探究这些差异的真正来源。

Method: 采用无添加剂水相共沉淀法，以K4[Fe(CN)6]分别与FeCl3、CoCl2、NiCl2反应合成Fe-PBA、Co-PBA、Ni-PBA。通过X射线衍射（XRD）、衰减全反射红外光谱（ATR-FTIR）、拉曼光谱、场发射扫描电镜/能谱（FEG-SEM/EDS）及热重/差热分析（TG/SDTA）对三种粉末进行系统对比表征。

Result: 三种组成均呈现特征立方氰桥PBA框架，表观晶格参数分别为Fe-PBA 10.10±0.02 Å、Co-PBA 10.01±0.02 Å、Ni-PBA 10.10±0.03 Å。ATR-FTIR中主C≡N伸缩振动峰沿Fe-Co-Ni序列由2062 cm⁻¹升至2071、2087 cm⁻¹，表明局部氰根环境随组成变化。Fe-PBA含钾量低于Co、Ni粉末，但在低温区呈现明显更大的质量损失。三种衍射图谱中均观察到归属于晶态KCl的尖锐反射，而Ni-PBA还出现未指认的额外反射，表明存在至少一种其他晶相。

Conclusion: Fe、Co、Ni基PBA之间的差异不能仅归因于过渡金属种类，前驱体氧化态和洗涤效率同样影响所得粉末的组成与热响应行为。这一认识对PBA材料的合成优化与性能调控具有指导意义。

Abstract: Prussian Blue (Fe-PBA) and its Co and Ni analogues (Co-PBA and Ni-PBA) were synthesized by an additive-free aqueous co-precipitation route using K$_4$[Fe(CN)$_6$] and FeCl$_3$, CoCl$_2$, or NiCl$_2$, respectively. All compositions exhibit the characteristic cubic cyanide-bridged PBA framework, with apparent lattice parameters of 10.10 +/- 0.02, 10.01 +/- 0.02, and 10.10 +/- 0.03 Angstrom for Fe-PBA, Co-PBA, and Ni-PBA, respectively. The dominant C$\equiv$N stretching band in ATR-FTIR shifts from 2062 to 2071 and 2087 cm$^{-1}$ across the Fe-Co-Ni series, indicating composition-dependent changes in the local cyanide environment. Fe-PBA contains less potassium than the Co- and Ni-containing powders and exhibits a substantially larger low-temperature mass loss. Sharp reflections assigned to crystalline KCl are observed in all three diffraction patterns, while additional unassigned reflections in Ni-PBA indicate the presence of at least one further crystalline phase. These results show that differences among Fe-, Co-, and Ni-based PBAs cannot be attributed solely to transition-metal identity, as precursor oxidation state and washing efficiency also influence the composition and thermal response of the resulting powders.

### [95] [Benchmarking Universal Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials for Temperature-Dependent Elasticity of Binary and High-Entropy Refractory Carbides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11878)
*Miroslav Lebeda,Jan Drahokoupil,Šimon Svoboda,Petr Vlčák*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文对9种通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）在二元及高熵难熔碳化物（300–1200 K）有限温度弹性性能预测上的准确性进行了基准测试，发现MACE-MH-1总体误差最低（5.7%），且绝对刚度和热软化需分别评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 难熔碳化物高温弹性性能的准确预测至关重要，但从头算分子动力学（AIMD）计算成本高昂，限制了跨组分和温度的系统性研究。uMLIPs提供高效替代方案，但其在此任务上的准确性尚缺乏充分验证。

Method: 针对5种二元碳化物和2种高熵碳化物，在300–1200 K范围内以一致的AIMD数据为参考，对9种uMLIPs进行基准测试。采用应力-应变分子动力学获取弹性常数及体模量、剪切模量和杨氏模量，显式采样热原子运动与非谐效应（超越单纯热膨胀）。分别评估绝对弹性性能和归一化热软化。

Result: MACE-MH-1总体平均绝对百分比误差最低（5.7%）；MACE-MH-1与DPA4-Mini对热软化再现最准确，平均偏差分别为2.5和2.3个百分点。所有模型普遍低估刚度，多数模型可与相对AIMD参考更大的平衡体积相关。C12的模型依赖性误差最大。高熵碳化物描述精度与二元碳化物相当，表明化学复杂性未带来明显精度损失；精度随过渡金属组分变化，V族碳化物（尤其TaC）最具挑战性。

Conclusion: 该研究筛选出适用于碳化物有限温度弹性的有前景的预训练模型，并指出绝对刚度与热软化的准确性必须独立评估，为高温条件下难熔碳化物的高效可靠评估提供了依据。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of temperature-dependent elasticity is important for assessing refractory carbides under high-temperature conditions, but the computational cost of ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) limits systematic investigations across compositions and temperatures. Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) offer an efficient alternative, yet their accuracy for this task remains insufficiently established. Here, we benchmark nine uMLIPs against consistent AIMD reference data for five binary and two high-entropy (HE) carbides between 300 and 1200 K. Elastic constants and the corresponding bulk, shear, and Young's moduli are obtained using stress-strain molecular dynamics, explicitly sampling thermal atomic motion and anharmonic effects beyond thermal expansion alone. We assess absolute elastic properties and normalized thermal softening separately. MACE-MH-1 achieves the lowest overall mean absolute percentage error (5.7%). MACE-MH-1 and DPA4-Mini reproduce thermal softening most accurately, with mean deviations of 2.5 and 2.3 percentage points, respectively. All models generally underestimate stiffness, with larger equilibrium volumes relative to the AIMD reference likely contributing to this trend for most models. C12 exhibits the largest model-dependent errors. The HE carbides are described with accuracy comparable to that of the binary carbides, indicating no apparent accuracy penalty from chemical complexity. Accuracy instead varies with transition-metal composition, with group-V carbides, particularly TaC, presenting the greatest challenge. These results identify promising pretrained models for finite-temperature elasticity in carbides and show why accurate absolute stiffness and thermal softening must be assessed independently.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond special quasirandom structures: free energies from energy cumulants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11882)
*Yann L. Müller,Anirudh Raju Natarajan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于能量累积量(cumulants)展开的自由能计算方法，将传统"特殊准随机结构能量+理想组态熵"近似推广为无穷级数的一阶项；高阶累积量仅需随机排布的能量即可估计，无需蒙特卡洛或分子动力学模拟，可将无序相的相图、缺陷浓度与短程序纳入常规高通量筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测无序相的有限温度稳定性需要其自由能，但传统方法用特殊准随机结构能量加理想组态熵来近似，忽略了冷却过程中形成的短程序，导致自由能与相图预测存在误差。

Method: 将自由能按能量累积量作无穷级数展开，传统近似是该展开的首项；高阶累积量直接从随机排布构型的能量中估计，无需蒙特卡洛或分子动力学模拟。方法适用于任意晶格与化学组成。

Result: 在九元难熔合金体系中，加入二阶累积量后自由能误差降低约一个数量级；展开式能给出有序-无序转变相图、缺陷浓度和短程序。并利用基础原子间势对26种元素的富锂岩盐氧化物进行筛选，识别出富含Li4团簇（锂渗流所需）的可合成无序相。

Conclusion: 该能量累积量展开方法对任意晶格和化学体系通用，能把无序相纳入常规材料筛选流程，显著提升无序相自由能与相图预测的精度和效率。

Abstract: Predicting the finite-temperature stability of a disordered phase requires its free energy. It is traditionally approximated by combining the energy of a special quasirandom structure (a small cell mimicking a random alloy) with the ideal configurational entropy. This approximation neglects the short-range order that develops on cooling. In this study, we show that the conventional approximation is the first term of an infinite expansion of the free energy in energy cumulants. Higher-order cumulants are estimated from energies of random arrangements, without Monte Carlo or molecular dynamics. In a nine-element refractory system, adding the second cumulant reduced free energy errors by about an order of magnitude. The expansion gives phase diagrams with order--disorder transitions, defect concentrations, and short-range order. To demonstrate its utility, we used a foundation interatomic potential to screen lithium-excess rocksalt oxides across 26 elements for synthesizable disordered phases rich in the Li$_4$ clusters needed for lithium percolation. The approach applies to any lattice and chemistry, and brings disordered phases within reach of routine screening.

</details>


### [97] [Comprehensive study of massively overlapping cascades in common elemental metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11910)
*Aslak Fellman,Jesper Byggmästar,Fredric Granberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文对21种金属进行了大规模重叠级联的分子动力学模拟，发现模拟结果对原子间势的选择高度敏感，并系统总结了FCC、BCC、HCP三类晶体结构在辐照下形成的典型缺陷类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究辐照损伤（如聚变堆、裂变堆材料）需要理解高剂量下重叠级联产生的复杂缺陷结构。以往研究多为单级联或少数元素，缺乏对多种元素在统一框架下的大规模重叠级联模拟，且不同原子间势（经典解析势与机器学习势）对结果的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 对Be、Al、Ti、V、Cr、Fe、Co、Ni、Cu、Zr、Nb、Mo、Rh、Pd、Ag、Hf、Ta、W、Pt、Au、Pb共21种元素（覆盖FCC、BCC、HCP结构）各进行2000次累积的5 keV级联分子动力学模拟；同时使用多种经典解析原子间势及机器学习原子间势（对大部分元素）进行对照，并分析饱和缺陷浓度与材料基本物理性质的相关性。

Result: 大规模重叠级联模拟结果在整个周期表范围内对原子间势的选择非常敏感。FCC金属均因辐照形成堆垛层错四面体（SFT），并通常包含间隙型Shockley不全位错和Frank型位错；BCC材料通常形成间隙型1/2⟨111⟩位错，但V和Nb中还观察到空位型1/2⟨111⟩位错；HCP材料倾向于形成以a型位错为主的复杂位错结构，既可为空位型也可为间隙型。

Conclusion: 重叠级联缺陷形成强烈依赖于原子间势，表明现有势函数在辐照损伤模拟中具有不确定性；缺陷类型与晶体结构存在系统性对应关系（FCC-SFT、BCC-1/2⟨111⟩位错、HCP-a型位错），并初步探索了饱和缺陷浓度与基础材料性质的关联。

Abstract: Massively overlapping cascades simulations were carried out in 21 elemental metals: Be, Al, Ti, V, Cr, Fe, Co, Ni, Cu, Zr, Nb, Mo, Rh, Pd, Ag, Hf, Ta, W, Pt, Au and Pb. These elements have simple FCC, BCC and HCP lattice structures. For each element, 2000 cumulative 5 keV cascades were simulated using molecular dynamics. The overlapping cascades were simulated using various classical analytical interatomic potentials as well as with machine-learning interatomic potentials for many of the elements. The general conclusion is that results from massively overlapping cascades simulations are very sensitive to the choice of interatomic potential across the periodic table. Furthermore, we see that FCC metals all form stacking fault tetrahedra due to irradiation, and also typically include interstitial type Shockley partial and Frank type dislocations. In BCC materials typically form interstitial 1/2$\langle$1 1 1$\rangle$ dislocations, but vacancy type 1/2$\langle$1 1 1$\rangle$ dislocations were also observed in V and Nb. HCP materials tend to form complex dislocation structures mainly consisting of a-type dislocations that can be of both vacancy or interstitial type. Correlations between saturated defect concentrations and fundamental underlying material properties are explored.

</details>


### [98] [Homogeneous Charge Relaxation in Organic Mixed Ionic-Electronic Conductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11977)
*Silan Zhang,Scott T. Keene,Junfu Tian,Georgina E. Lindop,Simon M. Fairclough,Zhengkang Qu,Xian Wei Chua,Christopher Slaughter,Christina J. Kousseff,Caterina Ducati,Iain McCulloch,Henning Sirringhaus,Akshay Rao,George G. Malliaras*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用操作电荷光度法追踪了有机混合离子-电子导体（OMIEC）薄膜在偏置及开路弛豫过程中的电荷行为，发现偏置下电荷以氧化还原前沿移动，去偏置后电荷在整个通道内均匀弛豫；结合建模与冷冻电镜证明溶胀形成的连通富水通路既加速离子输运又导致存储电荷泄漏，并通过调控聚噻吩末端侧链实现稳定可编程态与快速电生理传感的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OMIEC 能耦合离子与电子信号，可用于储能、神经形态计算和生物电子学，但去除偏置后电荷如何在水中弛豫（即存储态能维持多久）尚不清楚，这限制了器件的保持性能与设计优化。

Method: 采用操作电荷光度法（operando charge photometry）实时追踪 OMIEC 薄膜在偏置施加和开路弛豫过程中的电荷分布；结合建模与冷冻电子显微镜（cryo-EM）表征溶胀后体相内的连通富水通路；并通过改变聚噻吩末端侧链（疏水大体积侧链 vs 亲水侧链）调控吸水量与离子捕获能力。

Result: 偏置下电荷以氧化还原前沿形式移动；去除偏置后电荷在整个通道内均匀弛豫。溶胀在体相中形成连通的富水通路，这些通路既加快离子运动，又使存储电荷更容易泄漏。大体积疏水侧链能够实现稳定的可编程状态，而亲水侧链则支持快速电生理传感。

Conclusion: 溶胀诱导的连通富水通路是决定 OMIEC 存储态保持时间的关键因素；通过调控聚噻吩末端侧链可权衡电荷保持与离子响应速度，为设计兼具稳定可编程性与快速传感能力的 OMIEC 器件提供指导。

Abstract: Organic mixed ionic-electronic conductors (OMIECs) couple ionic and electronic signals, enabling energy storage, neuromorphic computing, and bioelectronics. However, how charge relaxes in water once the bias is removed, which determines how long stored states persist, remains unclear. Using operando charge photometry, we tracked charge in OMIEC films during biasing and open-circuit relaxation. Under bias, charges moved as redox fronts. After bias removal, charge instead relaxed uniformly across the channel. Modeling and cryo-electron microscopy indicate that swelling creates connected water-rich pathways through the bulk. These pathways speed ion motion but also let stored charge leak away. Tuning the terminal side chain of a polythiophene controls water uptake and ion trapping. Bulky hydrophobic side chains enable stable programmable states, whereas hydrophilic side chains support fast electrophysiology sensing.

</details>


### [99] [Multifunctional synaptic learning and neuromorphic computing using crystalline TiO$_x$/NiO$_x$ heterojunction-based memory devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11992)
*Dip Manna,Koushik Mondal,Subhajit Karmakar,Pawan Kumar Ojha,Sourav Roy,Biswarup Satpati,Prasanta Karmakar,Supratic Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究利用Ag/TiOx/NiOx/p++-Si忆阻器结构模拟生物突触的多种特性，发现NiOx/TiOx异质结是实现可重复模拟的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过一种基于Ag/TiOx/NiOx/p++-Si的忆阻器结构，模拟生物突触的多种特性，以探索其在神经形态计算和类脑器件中的应用潜力。

Method: 研究Ag/TiOx/NiOx/p++-Si忆阻器在直流和脉冲偏置下的不同输运特性；利用晶粒较小的晶态NiOx促进其上层TiOx形成晶态且晶粒更大的薄膜；该异质结构在反向偏置下表现出稳定的免电铸(forming free)双极非易失性阻变特性，并具有渐变的置位(set)和复位(reset)特征。

Result: 器件成功实现了经典巴甫洛夫学习、人工伤害感受器(nociceptor)模拟以及摩尔斯电码检测；将实测电导-编程脉冲关系得出的突触权重用于人工神经网络，在MNIST手写数字识别中取得近95%的准确率。

Conclusion: NiOx/TiOx异质结在获得可重复的I-V特性方面起关键作用，使该器件能够有效模拟生物突触的多种功能，展现出良好的神经形态器件应用前景。

Abstract: An attempt is made here to mimic different properties of biological synapses using Ag/TiO$_x$/NiO$_x$/p$^{++}$-Si memristor structure by studying its different transport properties under dc and pulsed bias. The presence of crystalline NiO$_x$ with smaller gains is found to be helpful to get TiO$_x$ deposited on its top with crystalline properties and larger grains. The heterostructure offers stable bipolar forming free non-volatile resistive switching characteristics under reverse biased condition with gradual set and reset features. These devices are also able to successfully implement the classical Pavlov's learning, study artificial nociceptor and Morse code detection. While incorporating synaptic weights derived from the measured conductance programming pulse relationship, an artificial neural network achieves nearly 95\% accuracy in MNIST digit recognition. In brief, NiO$_x$/TiO$_x$ heterojunction plays the pivotal role in getting such reproducible $I-V$ characteristics for mimicking different properties of biological synapses.

</details>


### [100] [Emergent gold-induced interfacial magnetism in monolayer FePS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12041)
*Giuseppe Buccoliero,Anita Polli,Marli dos Reis Cantarino,Rachel Nickel,Aloïs Arrighi,Roberto Sant,Tristan Riccardi,Valérie Reita,Nedjma Bendiab,Laëtitia Marty,Kurt Kummer,Johann Coraux,Nicholas B. Brookes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究二维反铁磁体 FePS₃ 与超薄金膜之间的邻近效应，发现金膜显著改变 FePS₃ 的磁性，诱导出可观界面磁矩。通过元素选择性 X 射线吸收和共振非弹性 X 射线散射等技术，作者逐层探测从单层到块体的磁性变化，揭示界面电荷转移和 Fe 3d-Au 杂化是磁性改变的微观机制，且该效应在五层时消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料因界面原子级洁净和极薄的特性，是强邻近效应的理想宿主，但通常衬底被视为被动支撑。作者希望探究金属衬底（尤其是金膜）能否主动重塑二维磁体 FePS₃ 的基态磁性，从而为层数加衬底工程提供原子级精确的调控手段。

Method: 采用金辅助剥离制备大面积 FePS₃ 薄片，结合元素选择性 X 射线吸收映射进行从单层到块体的逐层研究，并以惰性 SiO₂ 衬底作为对照。利用 X 射线磁圆二色性（XMCD）在 9 T、5 K 下测量轨道矩与自旋矩，用共振非弹性 X 射线散射（RIXS）探测晶体场（d-d）激发和电荷转移区光谱权重，并通过原子多重态计算加以佐证。

Result: 在 9 T、5 K 下，金上单层 FePS₃ 的 XMCD 信号比 SiO₂ 上增强三倍，轨道/自旋矩比值下降约 40%。RIXS 显示晶体场（d-d）激发崩塌，光谱权重大量转移至电荷转移区。原子多重态计算表明这些变化源于界面电荷转移和 Fe 3d-Au 杂化，该效应在头三层逐渐衰减，并在五层时完全消失。

Conclusion: 超薄金膜并非被动支撑，而是通过界面电荷转移和 Fe 3d-Au 杂化主动重塑 FePS₃ 的磁性，诱导出可观界面磁矩并大幅改变其轨道/自旋性质。这一效应具有明显的层数依赖性，确立了“层数+衬底工程”作为原子级精确调控二维磁体的有效途径。

Abstract: Owing to their atomically clean interfaces and extreme thinness, two-dimensional (2D) materials are ideal hosts for strong proximity effects, whereby contact with an adjacent material reshapes their ground state. Here, we demonstrate such an effect within a hybrid heterostructure, between the 2D antiferromagnet FePS$_3$ and an ultrathin gold film. Rather than a passive support, the latter profoundly changes the nature of the magnetism in the 2D magnet, inducing a sizeable interfacial moment. Large-area flakes from Au-assisted exfoliation, combined with element-selective X-ray absorption mapping, enable a layer-by-layer study from monolayer to bulk, benchmarked against an inert SiO$_2$ substrate reference. At 9 T and 5 K, the monolayer on gold shows a three-fold enhanced X-ray magnetic circular dichroism relative to SiO$_2$, with a $\sim$40% reduction of the orbital-to-spin moment ratio. Resonant inelastic X-ray scattering reveals a collapse of the crystal-field (d-d) excitations and a massive spectral-weight transfer to the charge-transfer region, unveiling the microscopic mechanism behind this change. Supported by atomic multiplet calculations, we identify interfacial charge transfer and Fe 3d-Au hybridization as the origin of these effects, which decay over the first three layers and vanish at the pentalayer, establishing layer number combined with substrate engineering as an atomically precise handle.

</details>


### [101] [Direct Quantification of Molecular Helicity-Dependent Effective Magnetic Fields in Chiral Polypeptide/Ferromagnet Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12145)
*Theo Balland,Buddhika Hondamuni,Shira Yochelis,Meital Ozeri,Simon Sochiera,Angela Wittmann,Yossi Paltiel,Dmitry Budker,Jonas Fransson,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究手性诱导的自旋选择性（CISS）效应，通过绝缘隔离层将吸附的手性多肽单分子层与铁磁多层膜解耦，利用磁光克尔效应显微镜定量测量了手性分子通过手性声子介导、作用于铁磁层的对映体依赖有效磁场，建立分子手性与磁性响应之间的直接定量联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前CISS研究主要关注手性结构对自旋输运的影响，而近期实验表明吸附的手性分子可影响相邻铁磁层的磁态，暗示存在手性诱导的长程磁相互作用，但其起源和定量表征尚属空白。

Method: 构建分子/铁磁多层膜混合异质结构，用绝缘隔离层将手性多肽自组装单分子层与磁体解耦以证明长程性；利用磁光克尔效应（MOKE）显微镜，结合无分子覆盖的片上参考区域，精确测量局域磁滞回线的可重复位移，直接定量提取对映体依赖的有效磁场。

Result: 手性螺旋结构多肽可通过绝缘层对铁磁多层膜施加对映体依赖的有效磁场（可能由手性声子介导）；提取的有效磁场对相反手性的对映体反号，并与对映体过量（enantiomeric excess）相关。

Conclusion: 这些结果在混合异质结构中建立了分子手性与磁响应之间的直接定量联系，为CISS相关效应提供了一个可通过实验访问的基准。

Abstract: Chirality-Induced Spin Selectivity (CISS) refers to structural chirality governing spin transport in chiral systems, with experimental signatures reported in both transport experiments and photoemission. Beyond influencing transport, recent experiments indicate that adsorbed chiral molecules can influence the magnetic state of adjacent ferromagnetic layers, suggesting the existence of chirality-induced effects whose origin and quantitative characterization remain lacking. Here, we investigate chirality-induced long-range magnetic interactions in a hybrid molecular/ferromagnetic multilayer beyond the conventional direct transport coupling. We demonstrate the long-range nature by decoupling the adsorbed chiral molecules from the magnet using an insulating spacer. We show that self-assembled monolayers of chiral polypeptides with a helical structure can act on a ferromagnetic multilayer via an enantiomer-dependent effective magnetic field that may be mediated by chiral phonons through the insulating layer. Using Magneto-Optical Kerr Effect (MOKE) microscopy with on-chip reference regions free of molecules, we directly quantify this field by measuring precisely the reproducible shifts of the local hysteresis loops. The extracted effective field reverses sign for opposite enantiomers and correlates with the enantiomeric excess. These results provide a direct and quantitative link between molecular chirality and magnetic response in hybrid heterostructures, establishing an experimentally accessible benchmark for a CISS-related effect.

</details>


### [102] [Specialized machine learning force fields for materials dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12151)
*Yue Wu,Haoyu Wan,Yuan Tian,Deng Pan,Mingwei Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本综述探讨如何通过物理驱动的专业化方法扩展机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）在复杂材料动力学中的适用性，分析了MLIP设计中的结构性权衡，并通过四个应用场景建立可观测量验证框架，最终提出物理一致、硬件感知力场的发展路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预训练的等变图神经网络在近平衡性质上具有稳健的零样本性能，但其向复杂材料动力学的迁移仍受分布外反应态、表示偏差和计算扩展限制的根本性挑战，亟需物理驱动的专业化来突破这些瓶颈。

Method: 系统评估MLIP设计中的结构性权衡（高应变构型的欠采样、高阶消息传递架构的高计算开销、开放与场耦合系统对非局域相互作用的需求），并通过四个要求严苛的应用场景（电化学界面、成分波动的开放系统、多相演化、大规模断裂）建立面向可观测量的验证框架。

Result: 得出通用基础模型与任务特定势能互补的结论，强调针对性适配必须针对预期可观测物理量进行严格基准测试。

Conclusion: 提出开发物理一致、硬件感知的力场路线图，以无缝衔接电子结构精度与宏观材料现象。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are transforming atomistic simulations by accessing unprecedented length and time scales. While pretrained equivariant graph neural networks achieve robust zero-shot performance for near-equilibrium properties across broad chemical spaces, their translation to complex materials dynamics remains fundamentally challenged by out-of-distribution reactive states, representation biases, and computational scaling limits. In this Review, we examine how physics-driven specialization extends the applicability of MLIPs to complex dynamical systems. We systematically evaluate the structural trade-offs in MLIP design: the undersampling of highly strained configurations, the prohibitive computational overhead of high-order message-passing architectures, and the necessity of nonlocal interactions for open and field-coupled systems. Through four demanding application contexts - electrified interfaces, compositionally fluctuating open systems, multiphase evolution, and large-scale fracture - we establish a framework for observable-specific validation. Highlighting the complementary roles of universal foundation models and task-specific potentials, we emphasize that targeted adaptations must be rigorously benchmarked against intended observables. We conclude with a roadmap for developing physically consistent, hardware-aware force fields that seamlessly connect electronic-structure accuracy to macroscopic materials phenomena.

</details>


### [103] [A Universal Polybromide Melt Strategy for the Direct Conversion of Metals into Optoelectronic-Grade Bromide Perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12278)
*Arindam Mondal,Mark Baranov,Lonia R. Friedlander,Mariela J. Pavan,Kirill Sobolev Yevgeny Rakita*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用、单步、无有机溶剂的合成平台，利用甲铵多溴化物熔体（PBM）在室温下直接与元素金属（Pb、Sn、Au、Cu）反应，将其转化为相应的溴化物钙钛矿及相关相，并成功抑制了Sn的过氧化，实现高质量MASnBr₃薄膜的常温制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卤化物钙钛矿合成要求特定的金属氧化态（M²⁺或M⁺/M³⁺对），限制了加工灵活性，且对Sn、Cu、Au等多价金属存在稳定性挑战，亟需一种能直接从稳定元素金属出发、避开复杂氧化态调控的合成范式。

Method: 开发一种通用、单步、无有机溶剂的合成平台：使用甲铵多溴化物熔体（PBM）在室温下与不同元素金属（Pb、Sn、Au、Cu）反应，分别生成MAPbBr₃、MASnBr₃、MA₂Au₂Br₆和MA₂CuBr₄。通过调节PBM的化学活性并结合Sn金属介导的comproportionation（归中/互变价）反应，抑制Sn向Sn⁴⁺的过氧化；并利用原位X射线衍射捕获相演化过程中的瞬态中间相。

Result: 成功将Pb、Sn、Au、Cu四种元素金属直接转化为各自的Br钙钛矿相；通过调节PBM组成并结合金属Sn介导的归中反应，在环境条件下抑制了Sn过氧化并制得高质量MASnBr₃薄膜；原位XRD在两种情况下捕捉到瞬态中间相；氧化与歧化电位被证实可用于预测M:PBM反应结果。

Conclusion: 该合成平台提供了一种从稳定元素金属直接制备钙钛矿的低门槛途径，氧化与歧化电位可作为反应结果预测指标，且与卷对卷基础设施完全兼容，为规模化升级下一代光电子技术铺平道路。

Abstract: Halide perovskite systems require specific metal oxidation states (M2+ or M+/M3+ pairs), limiting processing flexibility during traditional synthesis and posing stability challenges for multivalent metals like Sn, Cu, and Au. This work anchors a profound paradigm shift: a universal, single-step, organic-solvent free synthetic platform that directly converts stable elemental metal films into bromide perovskite and perovskite-related systems for a wide variety of (opto)electronic applications. The synthetic platform uses methylammonium polybromide melt (PBM) that reacts at room temperature with different elemental metals, including Pb, Sn, Au, and Cu, successfully transforming them into their respective Br-perovskite phases: MAPbBr3, MASnBr3, MA2Au2Br6, and MA2CuBr4. It is shown that the PBMs chemical activity can be tuned, and in situ X-ray diffraction captures in two cases transient intermediate phases during phase evolution. For Sn, which tends towards overoxidation to Sn4+ phase, changes in the PBMs composition coupled with a metallic Sn mediated comproportionation reaction allowed suppressing overoxidation, and formation of high quality MASnBr3 films under ambient conditions. Overall, oxidation and disproportionation potentials are found to be useful metrics for predicting M:PBM reaction outcomes. Industrially, this approach should be fully compatible with roll-to-roll infrastructure, providing a low-barrier pathway to scale next-generation optoelectronic technologies.

</details>


### [104] [The Stability of Rutile Oxides for Oxygen Evolution Catalysis: From Mechanistic Understanding to New Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12297)
*Katarina Kretschmer,Michael H. Eikerling,Tobias Binninger*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过计算分析金红石型IrO2、RuO2及若干MO2体系的晶体轨道电子结构，区分金属-氧键中的共价与离子贡献，从电子层面解释IrO2与RuO2在析氧反应中稳定性差异的根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物电解质水电解是绿色制氢的关键技术，IrO2是阳极析氧反应（OER）的基准电催化剂，但铱稀缺昂贵，亟需寻找耐用低成本替代品或大幅降低铱用量。RuO2活性相当但稳定性不如IrO2，本文旨在从电子结构层面揭示两者稳定性差异的根源，为理性设计替代催化剂提供依据。

Method: 对金红石型IrO2、RuO2及选定的MO2体系进行体相电子结构计算，通过对比分析，利用晶体轨道衍生的电子描述符区分金属-氧键中的共价贡献与离子贡献，从而定量剖析金属-氧键强度。

Result: 成功区分了IrO2与RuO2中金属-氧键的共价和离子成分，阐明了二者稳定性差异的电子起源，并据此为寻找兼具活性与稳定性的铱替代催化剂提供电子结构层面的判据。

Conclusion: IrO2相较RuO2更高的稳定性源于其金属-氧键中不同的共价/离子贡献，该电子结构分析框架可用于指导低铱或无铱OER催化剂的理性设计。

Abstract: Polymer electrolyte water electrolysis is considered as a pivotal technology for green hydrogen production. Iridium dioxide (IrO2) serves as the benchmark electrocatalyst for the anodic oxygen evolution reaction (OER) in these cells. However, the scarcity and cost of iridium drives efforts to find durable and cost-effective replacements or drastically reduce the iridium loading. While ruthenium dioxide (RuO2) offers comparable activity, its practical utility is impacted by its stability being inferior to IrO2. The presented computational study analyzes the metal-oxygen bond strengths in bulk IrO2 and RuO2 to provide insights into the electronic origins of their differing stabilities. Through a comparative analysis of the electronic structure of bulk rutile IrO2, RuO2, and selected MO2 systems, it is able to discriminate the covalent and ionic contributions to the metal-oxygen bond using crystal-orbital-derived electronic descriptors.

</details>

### [105] [Theory of Topologically Ordered Superfluids in 2+1 Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10668)
*Chao-Ming Jian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个刻画 (2+1) 维玻色拓扑有序超流体（TOSF）拓扑数据的一般框架：通过对偶规范化的双向映射，将 TOSF 的拓扑信息系统地编码为对称丰富拓扑序（SET）的代数数据，并据此研究涡旋的量子统计等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑有序超流体同时具有能隙的任意子激发和因 Goldstone 模式而保持无能隙的特性，但其拓扑数据的系统刻画缺乏一般框架，尤其是超流涡旋的拓扑性质难以直接从凝聚态描述中提取。

Method: 作者利用规范化（gauging）技巧建立双向映射：规范电荷 U(1) 对称将 TOSF 映射到具有对偶 U(1) 对称、霍尔电导为零的能隙拓扑序；再规范对偶 U(1) 即可恢复 TOSF。基于此双向映射，借助对称丰富拓扑序（SET）的代数框架编码 TOSF 的所有拓扑数据。同时发展了代数与场论两种互补表述，场论表述还自然给出通往能隙拓扑序相的相变路径。最后用 parton 构造给出具有非阿贝尔涡旋的玻色 TOSF 实例。

Result: 建立了 TOSF 拓扑数据与 SET 代数数据的系统对应关系；证明超流涡旋与能隙任意子部分中的剩余分立对称所对应的通量缺陷具有相同的拓扑性质；给出了代数与场论两种表述，并通过 parton 构造展示了非阿贝尔涡旋的玻色 TOSF 例子。

Conclusion: 通过双向规范映射，TOSF 的拓扑数据（凝聚电荷、去禁闭任意子内容、涡旋拓扑性质）可完全纳入对称丰富拓扑序的代数框架加以刻画，为理解与分类拓扑有序超流体及其相变提供了系统工具。

Abstract: Topologically ordered superfluids (TOSFs) support deconfined, gapped anyonic excitations while remaining gapless because of Goldstone modes. We develop a general framework for characterizing their topological data in $(2+1)$-dimensional bosonic systems with spontaneously broken charge ${\rm U}(1)_{\rm c}$ symmetry. Gauging ${\rm U}(1)_{\rm c}$ maps a TOSF to a gapped topological order enriched by a dual ${\rm U}(1)_{\rm dual}$ symmetry with vanishing Hall conductance, while gauging ${\rm U}(1)_{\rm dual}$ recovers the TOSF. This two-way mapping allows the topological data of the TOSF, including the condensate charge, deconfined anyon content, and topological properties of vortices, to be systematically encoded using the algebraic framework of symmetry-enriched topological orders. In particular, superfluid vortices share the topological properties of flux defects associated with the residual discrete symmetry in the gapped anyon sector. We develop complementary algebraic and field-theoretic formulations of this framework, with the latter also suggesting a natural transition to a gapped topologically ordered phase. Additionally, using parton constructions, we present examples of TOSFs with non-Abelian vortices in bosonic systems.

</details>


### [106] [SC$^\sharp$: superconductivity intertwined with topological order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10679)
*Zhi-Qiang Gao,Yan-Qi Wang,Hui Yang,Zhaoyu Han,Ashvin Vishwanath*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文为(2+1)D带隙超导体建立了一个基于涡旋拓扑性质的分类框架，重点刻画超导性与内禀拓扑序的相互交织，并发现了全新的一类SC$^{\sharp}$相，其基元涡旋必然非阿贝尔且不可分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导性与内禀拓扑序的共存方式不止于简单叠加，二者可以更深层次地相互交织。现有分类（仅可逆超导体、SC$^\ast$等）无法完整刻画这种交织，尤其是涡旋的拓扑性质如何反映并约束这种耦合仍不清楚。

Method: 作者聚焦于凝聚电荷为$2ne$的带隙超导体中涡旋的拓扑性质，建立代数分类框架，并通过任意子凝聚(anyon condensation)系统地构造这些超导相。据此按交织程度将其分为可逆超导体、untwisted/twisted SC$^\ast$以及新发现的SC$^{\sharp}$。

Result: 主要发现是SC$^{\sharp}$族：携带$h/(2ne)$磁通的基元涡旋无法分解为"可逆超导体涡旋+体任意子"的乘积，且每个基元涡旋必然非阿贝尔。作者还得到否定性结论：基元涡旋的量子维数至少为$\sqrt{6}$，且不能满足Fibonacci融合规则。进一步引入primitive SC$^\sharp$相，其所有非平凡涡旋扇区的分解都被阻碍，并论证这类相不能通过对更低电荷超导凝聚体相对相位的无序化来产生。

Conclusion: 基于涡旋性质的分类方案揭示了拓扑有序超导体之间新的区别，并约束了它们的可实现方式，为理解超导与拓扑序的深层交织提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Superconductivity and intrinsic topological order can intertwine in ways that go beyond simple coexistence. Here we develop a classification framework for (2+1)D gapped superconductors with condensate charge $2ne$. By focusing on the topological properties of superconducting vortices, we precisely characterize this interplay. We obtain a classification with increasing levels of intertwinement, from the well known invertible superconductors without topological order, to untwisted and twisted SC$^\ast$, and finally to SC$^{\sharp}$. Our main finding is the family SC$^{\sharp}$, in which the elementary vortex carrying $h/(2ne)$ flux cannot be factorized into an invertible superconductor vortex dressed by bulk anyons, and every elementary vortex is necessarily non-Abelian. For such phases, we also obtain no-go results: the elementary vortex sector has quantum dimension at least $\sqrt{6}$ and cannot satisfy Fibonacci fusion rules. We develop a systematic algebraic framework to obtain all these superconducting phases through anyon condensation, allowing us to construct several infinite families of examples and make connections to anyon superconductivity. Finally, we introduce primitive SC$^\sharp$ phases for which factorization of every nontrivial vortex sector is obstructed, and argue that they cannot arise by disordering the relative phases of lower-charge superconducting condensates. Our classification scheme based on vortex properties allows us to uncover new distinctions between topologically ordered superconductors and constrain how they can be realized.

</details>


### [107] [Spin Chain with Continuous Exponential Symmetry: Topological Phase and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10682)
*Biao Lian,Zhen Bi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究具有连续指数对称性 $U(1)\rtimes\mathbb Z_2$（整数基 $q$）的一维自旋 $S$ 链的对称有隙基态。结果表明，整数 $S$ 下奇数 $q$ 允许 $\mathbb Z_2$ Haldane 型 SPT 相，偶数 $q$ 不允许；半奇整数 $S$ 下奇数 $q$ 因 LSM 阻碍禁止唯一对称有隙基态，偶数 $q$ 则强制出现一种仅单边有受保护边缘模、且只有一半弦序的新“半 SPT”相，超出既有分类框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在具有连续晶格对称性（且 U(1) 电荷随位点指数衰减的调制对称性）条件下，一维量子自旋链中可能存在的对称保护拓扑相，以揭示超出传统分类方案的新型有隙相。

Method: 以量子破缺模型作为 XXZ Haldane 自旋链的 $q$-形变，研究具有 $U(1)\rtimes\mathbb Z_2$ 连续指数对称性的自旋 $S$ 链，系统分析不同 $S$（整数/半奇整数）与 $q$（奇/偶）组合下的对称基态拓扑，运用 SPT 分类、边缘模分析和 Lieb-Schultz-Mattis 阻碍论证。

Result: 整数 $S$：奇数 $q$ 允许非平凡 $\mathbb Z_2$ Haldane 型 SPT 相（具有受保护边缘模），偶数 $q$ 无 SPT 相；半奇整数 $S$：奇数 $q$ 因 LSM 阻碍禁止唯一对称有隙基态，偶数 $q$ 强制出现新型“半 SPT”相，该相仅在一侧边缘有受保护边缘模，并只具有 SPT 相一半的弦序。

Conclusion: 揭示了依赖 $S$ 与 $q$ 的拓扑相结构，并发现一种超出既有分类方案的新型对称保护有隙相（半 SPT 相），拓展了对一维量子自旋链中 SPT 相的理解。

Abstract: We study symmetric gapped ground states of one-dimensional spin $S$ chains with a continuous exponential symmetry $U(1)\rtimes\mathbb Z_2$ with integer base $q$, where the U(1) charge has a modulated unit of charge $q^{L-j}$ decaying exponentially with site $j$. The simplest model is a quantum breakdown model that can be viewed as a $q$-deformation of the XXZ Haldane spin chain. We show that the topology of their symmetric ground states crucially depends on $S$ and $q$. For integer $S$, odd $q$ allows a nontrivial $\mathbb Z_2$ Haldane-type symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phase with protected edge modes, while even $q$ allows no nontrivial SPT phase. For half-odd integer $S$, odd $q$ prohibits any unique symmetric gapped ground state due to a Lieb-Schultz-Mattis obstruction, whereas even $q$ enforces a novel symmetric gapped phase which we name as the ``half-SPT" phase. Unconventionally, the half-SPT phase only has a symmetry protected edge mode on one edge, and has half of the string orders of an SPT phase. Our results uncover a new type of symmetry-protected gapped phases beyond existing classification schemes.

</details>


### [108] [Competing symmetry breaking and topology in quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10694)
*Anthony Rey,Ömer M. Aksoy,Claudio Chamon,Akira Furusaki,Christopher Mudry*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究一维自旋-1/2系统中自发对称性破缺与对称性保护拓扑（SPT）之间的竞争与合作，构造了两类带Z2×Z2×Z2^T对称性的局域哈密顿量，发现由六种长程有序有能隙相、两种SPT伙伴相及两种无能隙相组成的丰富相图，并揭示了量子临界与多临界现象中对称性破缺与拓扑协同产生的新型临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自发对称性破缺与对称性保护拓扑在一维量子自旋-1/2系统中如何相互竞争与协作，特别是理解有能隙破缺相之间连续相变、无能隙边界自旋的出现，以及低维系统中连续对称性破缺与Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner-Coleman定理表面矛盾的物理机制。

Method: 作者构造两族具有Z2×Z2×Z2^T（时间反演）对称性的局域自旋-1/2哈密顿量，系统分析其基态相图与相变。相的分类依据Z2对称性破缺模式（FM与Neel、x/y/z方向）、是否有对称性保护的边界自由度（SPT）以及是否有能隙。利用Tomonaga-Luttinger液体理论、共形场论（中心荷c=1）和临界点分析刻画连续相变线与多临界点。

Result: 得到一类丰富的相图：六个长程有序有能隙相（FM_x,y,z与Neel_x,y,z）、两个SPT伙伴相（FM^SPT_x,y与Neel^SPT_x,y），以及两个无能隙相（gFM_z与gNeel_z），后者具有非公度Z2^T破缺的磁长程序且中心荷c=1。竞争有能隙相之间的直接相变可以连续或弱一阶。连续相变线呈现奇异临界性质：(1) Neel_x与Neel_y之间的解禁闭量子临界点线由Tomonaga-Luttinger液体理论描述，并在Lifshitz三临界点处被gFM_z相终止——该点虽在局域一维哈密顿量中，却表现出连续U(1)对称性的自发破缺，与Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner-Coleman定理的朴素预期相悖；(2) 不同Z2破缺的SPT相之间（如Neel^SPT_x与Neel^SPT_y）的临界线保留低能边界自由度，实现对称性富化的临界性（即无能隙SPT相）。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了竞争性对称性破缺与拓扑通过非传统机制生成有序临界相与多临界性：解禁闭量子临界线、Lifshitz三临界点以及对称性富化（无能隙SPT）临界性等。这些结果说明在一维系统中，对称性破缺与拓扑的协同可产生超出朴素维度约束（HMWC定理）的连续对称性自发破缺，并保留拓扑保护的边界低能自由度。

Abstract: We investigate how spontaneous symmetry breaking and symmetry-protected topology compete and cooperate in one-dimensional quantum spin-1/2 systems. We introduce two families of local Hamiltonians with $\mathbb{Z}^{\,}_{2}\times\mathbb{Z}^{\,}_{2}\times\mathbb{Z}^{T}_{2}$ symmetry which host a zoo of broken-symmetry phases with and without gapless boundary spins, namely, six long-range-ordered gapped phases that differ by their $\mathbb{Z}^{\,}_{2}$-symmetry-breaking patterns (FM$^{\,}_{x,y,z}$ and Neel$^{\,}_{x,y,z}$), their partner symmetry-protected topological phases (FM$^{\mathrm{SPT}}_{x,y}$ and Neel$^{\mathrm{SPT}}_{x,y}$), and two gapless phases (gFM$^{\,}_{z}$ and gNeel$^{\,}_{z}$) with incommensurate $\mathbb{Z}^{T}_{2}$-breaking magnetic long-range order and central charge $\mathsf{c}=1$. Direct transitions between competing gapped phases can be continuous or weakly first order. We demonstrate that continuous phase transition lines have exotic critical properties. For example, a line of deconfined quantum critical points (between the Neel$^{\,}_{x}$ and Neel$^{\,}_{y}$ phases) described by Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid theory is terminated by the gFM$^{\,}_{z}$ phase at a Lifshitz tricritical point which exhibits spontaneous breaking of the continuous U(1) symmetry despite the locality and one-dimensionality of the Hamiltonian, contrary to a naive expectation from the Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner-Coleman theorem. Furthermore, critical lines between symmetry-protected topological phases with different $\mathbb{Z}^{\,}_{2}$-symmetry breaking (e.g., Neel$^{\mathrm{SPT}}_{x}$ and Neel$^{\mathrm{SPT}}_{y}$) additionally retain low-energy boundary degrees of freedom, realizing symmetry-enriched criticality (or a gapless SPT phase). Our results reveal unconventional mechanisms by which competing symmetry breaking and topology generate ordered critical phases and multicriticality.

</details>


### [109] [Symmetric Mode Ansatz: Theory of Magnon Collective Modes in Chern Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10706)
*Miguel Gonçalves,Jonah Herzog-Arbeitman,Juan Felipe Mendez-Valderrama,Andreas Feuerpfeil,B. Andrei Bernevig,Nicolas Regnault*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种称为对称模式拟设（SyMA）的广义单模近似，用于描述莫尔材料（特别是扭曲MoTe2）中陈绝缘体磁体（含整数陈绝缘体与分数陈绝缘体）的低能磁振子谱，并在特定可解Hubbard模型中证明了零动量磁振子能隙的陈数、Berry曲率与量子度规双边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔材料中时间反演由平带铁磁性自发破缺，整数与分数陈绝缘体中相反自旋携带相反陈数，形成带隙磁振子激发的伊辛铁磁性。厘清这些集体模的性质及磁振子能隙的来源，对理解陈磁体稳定性至关重要，但尚缺乏一个准确且可解析的理论框架。

Method: 作者发展广义单模近似，即对称模式拟设（SyMA），描述低能磁振子谱；并引入两类可解析求解集体模的Hubbard模型族（包括Haldane模型与连续理想带），其中SyMA严格或精确近似成立。对该类中SyMA严格成立的模型，证明零动量磁振子能隙关于陈数、Berry曲率与量子度规的双边界的数学定理。

Result: SyMA在整数填充下与数值计算结果高度吻合，在分数填充（含分数陈绝缘体）上定性准确。通过可解模型揭示了SyMA高精度的根源，并证明了零动量磁振子能隙的上、下界（双边）由陈数、Berry曲率与量子度规决定，表明均匀量子几何足以稳定陈带中的磁性。

Conclusion: SyMA为理解陈磁体中带隙磁振子集体激发提供了精确而普适的框架，并首次建立了磁振子能隙与带几何（陈数、Berry曲率、量子度规）之间的定量联系，指出均匀量子几何是稳定陈带磁性的充分条件。

Abstract: Moiré materials host integer and fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) where time-reversal is broken spontaneously by flat band ferromagnetism. In twisted MoTe2, unlike in quantum Hall, opposite spins carry opposite Chern numbers and realize Ising ferromagnetism with gapped magnon excitations. Clarifying the nature of these collective modes and the origin of the magnon gap is key to understanding the stability of the Chern magnets in these materials. To do so, we develop a generalized single-mode approximation, which we call the symmetric mode ansatz (SyMA), to describe the low-energy magnon spectrum. We find it to be in exceptional agreement with numerical calculations at integer filling, and qualitatively accurate across fractional fillings including FCIs. To explain the accuracy of the SyMA, we present two families of Hubbard models (including the Haldane model and continuum ideal bands) whose collective modes are analytically solvable and for which the SyMA is either exact or an accurate approximation. For a class of these models where SyMA is exact, we prove two-sided bounds on the zero-momentum magnon gap in terms of the Chern number, Berry curvature, and quantum metric, revealing that uniform quantum geometry is sufficient for stabilizing magnetism in Chern bands.

</details>


### [110] [Cooper Instability of a Magnetic Wigner Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10710)
*Wangqian Miao,Chunli Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究菱方六层石墨烯中磁场下的电子晶体（磁性维格纳晶体）的集体模式能否通过声子机制介导电子配对。作者在哈特里-福克与含时哈特里-福克近似下求解两电子库珀问题，发现声子介导的吸引超过配对阈值，并产生手性 p 波配对不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验表明，在菱方六层石墨烯中，超导与电荷密度波序似乎源自邻近载流子密度处的同一自旋-谷极化铁磁金属，且扫描隧道显微镜直接观测到电子晶体。作者希望探究该磁性维格纳晶体的集体振动模式是否能够介导电子配对，从而在同一体系中实现电子结晶与超导。

Method: 作者在哈特里-福克（HF）与含时哈特里-福克（TDHF）近似下获得晶体的基态与集体模式能谱，并利用准玻色子近似导出电子-声子哈密顿量，进而求解第一导带中两电子的库珀问题。由于声子能量与费米子能带色散尺度相当、缺少绝热小参量，且体系破坏时间反演对称导致配对磁化率缺乏库珀对数发散，吸引必须超过有限阈值才能实现配对。

Result: 数值计算表明，声子介导的吸引强度超过了配对阈值，手性 p 波配对成为主要的（leading）不稳定性。这一结果说明，即使时间反演对称性破缺，维格纳晶体的集体模式仍能促成手性配对。

Conclusion: 在这些近似下，维格纳晶体的集体模式确实能够促成手性配对。该结果为建立研究电子结晶与超导的完整多体理论提供了令人鼓舞的起点。

Abstract: Recent scanning tunneling microscopy has directly imaged electronic crystals in rhombohedral hexalayer graphene, and magnetotransport measurements suggest that superconductivity and charge-density-wave order emerge from a common spin-valley-polarized ferromagnetic metal at nearby carrier densities. Motivated by these observations, we ask whether collective modes in a magnetic Wigner crystal can mediate pairing in this setting. We address this question by studying the Cooper problem for two electrons in the first conduction band of the Wigner crystal. Unlike conventional phonon-mediated pairing, the collective mode (boson) energies and single-particle (fermion) dispersion are comparable scales controlled by the electron density, leaving no small parameter for an adiabatic expansion. Moreover, because the magnetic Wigner crystal is not time-reversal invariant, the usual Cooper logarithmic divergence in the pairing susceptibility is absent, so the attraction must exceed a finite threshold for pairing to occur. These unusual features motivate a numerical experiment to see whether the crystal's own vibrations can bind electrons. We obtain the crystalline ground state and its collective mode spectrum within Hartree--Fock and time-dependent Hartree--Fock approximation, and derive the electron--phonon Hamiltonian using the quasiboson approximation. Solving the resulting two-electron problem, we find that phonon-mediated attraction exceeds the pairing threshold, with chiral $p$-wave pairing as the leading instability. Within these approximations, we show that collective modes of a Wigner crystal can indeed favor chiral pairing despite broken time-reversal symmetry. Our results provide an encouraging starting point for developing a full many-body theory to study electronic crystallization and superconductivity.

</details>


### [111] [Gauging Modulated Symmetries: Bond Algebras, Higher-Form Symmetries, and Symmetry-Enriched Topological Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10764)
*Gustavo M. Yoshitome,Pedro R. S. Gomes,Guilherme Delfino*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一个构造性框架，用于刻画 d 维格点系统中的调制对称性及其规范（gauging）。调制剖面被组织为作用在有限阿贝尔内部对称群上的空间群的有限维表示；由于相应自同构群有限，非平凡空间作用通过空间群的有限商来分解，从而给出周期性调制模式与离散阿贝尔调制对称性的分类。对称允许的局域相互作用由核构造系统给出，并同时产生开算子代数。规范后，这些元素被转化为扩展的 Wilson 算子和边界算子且保持代数关系；在退禁闭相，所得代数实现混合 't Hooft 反常，为阿贝尔晶体对称性富集拓扑相（SET）提供微观路径。这些 SET 不混合电、磁扇区，二者按互为反步（contragredient）的表示变换，从而保持其编织配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为调制对称性（modulated symmetries）建立系统的构造与分类框架，并阐明其规范化如何从微观上产生阿贝尔晶体对称性富集拓扑相（SET），同时解释文献中若干模型为何自然落入该框架。

Method: 将调制剖面组织为空间群在有限阿贝尔内部对称群上的有限维表示；利用相应自同构群有限这一事实，使非平凡空间作用通过空间群的有限商分解，得到周期性调制模式与分类。通过核（kernel）构造系统给出对称允许的局域相互作用，并生成开算子代数；随后执行规范（gauging），把开算子代数的元素转化为扩展的 Wilson 算子与边界算子并保持代数关系，在退禁闭相中实现混合 't Hooft 反常。

Result: 得到离散阿贝尔调制对称性的完整分类框架及对称允许相互作用、开算子代数的系统构造；规范后得到保持代数关系的扩展 Wilson/边界算子，并在退禁闭相实现混合 't Hooft 反常。所得 SET 中电、磁扇区不混合，按互为反步表示变换从而保持编织配对；框架能自然涵盖文献中多个已有模型。

Conclusion: 该构造框架为调制对称性及其规范提供了系统分类，并给出通向阿贝尔晶体 SET 的微观路径，同时统一了文献中的若干模型，展示了构造的普适性与实用性。

Abstract: We develop a constructive framework for characterizing modulated symmetries and their gauging in $d$-dimensional lattice systems. The modulation profiles are organized as a finite-dimensional representation of the space group acting on a finite Abelian internal symmetry group. Since the corresponding automorphism group is finite, the nontrivial spatial action factors through a finite quotient of the space group, leading to periodic modulation patterns and providing a classification framework for discrete Abelian modulated symmetries. The symmetry-allowed local interactions are systematically obtained from a kernel construction, which also yields an algebra of open operators. Upon gauging, its elements are transmuted into extended Wilson and boundary operators while retaining their algebraic relations. In the deconfined regime, the resulting algebra realizes a mixed 't Hooft anomaly, providing a microscopic route to Abelian crystalline symmetry-enriched topological phases (SETs). These SETs do not mix electric and magnetic sectors, which transform in mutually contragredient representations, thereby preserving their braiding pairing. We illustrate the framework through explicit examples and show that several models present in the literature fit naturally within our construction.

</details>


### [112] [Probing and Manipulating Quantum Materials with Strong-field Terahertz and Mid-infrared Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11126)
*S. J. Li,H. Y. Wu,S. J. Zhang,Y. T. Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用强场太赫兹（THz）和中红外（MIR）辐射探测与操控量子材料的最新进展，重点关注非线性电子动力学、集体模式耦合、对称性敏感响应及场诱导的电子结构与有序态变化，并特别介绍综合极端条件实验装置（SECUF）推动的相关研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料中的涌现现象源于不同自由度之间的相互作用，识别这些相互作用并确定其在有序态中的作用是凝聚态物理的核心问题。许多相关的低能激发和能隙位于THz和MIR频段，强相位稳定的强场既可以探测非线性动力学，又能将选定的自由度驱动至远离平衡态，这为揭示相互作用机理和控制材料性质提供了新途径。

Method: 作者采用综述方法，系统梳理强场THz和MIR辐射与量子材料相互作用的研究进展，涵盖以下核心方向：（1）非线性电子动力学；（2）集体模式耦合；（3）对称性敏感响应；（4）场诱导的电子结构和有序态变化。同时突出SECUF设施在极端条件下（如强场、低温、高压等综合条件）对上述研究的支撑作用。

Result: 强场THz/MIR技术能够有效探测并操控量子材料中位于该频段的低能激发与能隙，实现对特定自由度的选择性非平衡驱动，从而揭示不同自由度间的相互作用、激发集体模式耦合、检验对称性敏感响应，并诱导电子结构和有序态的改变。SECUF提供的综合极端条件显著拓展了这些研究的实验能力。

Conclusion: 强场THz和MIR辐射已成为识别量子材料中自由度相互作用、并实现材料性质主动调控的有力手段；结合SECUF等综合极端条件设施，该领域有望进一步深化对涌现现象机理的理解，并为量子材料的物性操控奠定基础。

Abstract: Emergent phenomena in quantum materials arise from interactions among different degrees of freedom. Identifying these interactions and establishing their roles in ordered states are central problems in condensed-matter physics and provide a basis for controlling material properties. Many relevant low-energy excitations and gaps lie in the terahertz (THz) and mid-infrared (MIR) range, where intense phase-stable fields can both interrogate nonlinear dynamics and drive selected degrees of freedom far from equilibrium. This review surveys recent progress in probing and manipulating quantum materials with strong-field THz and MIR radiation, with emphasis on nonlinear electronic dynamics, collective-mode coupling, symmetry-sensitive responses and field-induced changes in electronic structure and ordered states. Particular attention is given to developments enabled by the Synergetic Extreme Condition User Facility (SECUF).

</details>


### [113] [Multipolar fluctuations in localized $4f^2$-electron systems from dynamical mean-field theory: application to $\mathrm{PrCdNi}_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11562)
*Koki Numa,Junya Otsuki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文将基于密度泛函理论与动力学平均场理论的多极涨落分析从4f¹体系推广到4f²电子体系，并把该方法应用于PrCdNi₄，预言其具有(3z²-r²)型的反铁四极序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多极涨落分析方法最初是针对4f¹电子体系发展的，为了研究具有非Kramers双态等更复杂基态结构的4f²体系（如Pr基化合物）中的多极序与涨落，需要将该框架推广到f电子占据数n=2的情况。

Method: 作者在j-j耦合图像下，于j=5/2子空间内构造多粒子态，以复现4f²洪特规则基态多重态的晶体场劈裂；同时通过从单粒子基到多粒子基的映射，推导出4f²多重态间的多极磁化率与多极相互作用。随后将该框架应用于具有非Kramers Γ₃双态基态的PrCdNi₄。

Result: 在PrCdNi₄中，计算表明最近邻与第四近邻相互作用之间的竞争驱动了q=(2π,π,0)处的(3z²-r²)型反铁四极序。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将多极涨落分析扩展到4f²体系，并阐明了PrCdNi₄中反铁四极序的微观起源；所发展的形式体系可推广到一般的4fⁿ电子体系。

Abstract: Multipolar-fluctuation analysis based on density functional theory combined with dynamical mean-field theory is extended from $4f^1$- to $4f^2$-electron systems. Two methodological developments are presented. First, many-particle states are constructed within the $j$-$j$ coupling scheme in the $j=5/2$ subspace, so as to reproduce the crystalline-electric-field splitting of the $4f^2$ Hund's-rule ground-state multiplet. Second, multipolar susceptibilities and interactions between $4f^2$ multiplets are derived through a mapping from single-particle to many-particle bases. We apply this framework to PrCdNi$_4$, which has a non-Kramers $Γ_3$ doublet ground state, and propose a $(3z^2-r^2)$-type antiferroquadrupolar order at $\boldsymbol{q}=(2π,π,0)$ driven by the competition between nearest- and fourth-nearest-neighbor interactions. The formalism developed here is applicable to general $4f^n$-electron systems.

</details>


### [114] [Magnon band splitting without altermagnetism in CuF2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11838)
*Ioannis Rousochatzakis,Oleg Janson,Alexander A. Tsirlin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过计算与实验结合的方式，验证了金红石类材料 CuF₂ 并非此前预测的交替磁体，其真实单斜结构中交错磁性的能带劈裂被有效抑制，并揭示了强 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用等相对论效应的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确认交错磁（altermagnetic）态不能仅凭对称性论证，需要超越对称性分析，以真实材料 CuF₂ 为例说明仅凭对称性预测不可靠。

Method: 采用第一性原理（ab initio）计算与线性自旋波计算，并结合磁化强度测量，研究 CuF₂ 在实验单斜结构中的磁相互作用与自旋动力学。

Result: CuF₂ 可用弱耦合正方晶格层的自旋-1/2 模型描述：面内耦合 J₁≈115 K，两层面间反铁磁耦合分别为 J₁ 的 4% 和 8%。长程超交换驱动的倾斜层间耦合导致磁有序态晶胞加倍，从而有效抑制了交替磁能带劈裂。同时发现异常强的 DM 相互作用（|D|/J₁≈0.3），引起自旋倾斜，并与 order-by-disorder 效应共同将 Néel 矢量钉扎在晶格 b 轴。DM 各向异性引起磁振子能带劈裂，但这些能带保持非手性。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了即使在具有交替磁对称性的 3d 磁体中，相对论效应（如 DM 相互作用）依然至关重要，仅凭对称性分类不足以判定真实材料是否为交替磁体。

Abstract: Conclusive identification of an altermagnetic state requires going beyond mere symmetry arguments. We illustrate this in a combined computational and experimental study of the rutile-like material CuF$_2$, which is on the list of predicted altermagnets. Using ab initio and linear spin-wave calculations supplied by magnetization measurements, we show that CuF$_2$ in its experimental monoclinic structure can be described by a spin-$\frac12$ model of weakly coupled square-lattice layers with the in-plane coupling $J_1\simeq 115$ K and two synergistic antiferromagnetic interplane couplings amounting to 4% and 8% of $J_1$, respectively. Driven by long-range superexchange, these interlayer couplings are oblique to the square planes, resulting in the unit-cell doubling in the magnetically ordered state, thus effectively suppressing any altermagnetic band splitting. Concurrently, we identify unusually strong Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions, $|\mathbf D|/J_1\simeq 0.3$, that produce spin canting and, together with order-by-disorder effect, pin the Néel vector to the crystallographic $b$-axis. Additionally, DM anisotropy promotes magnon band splitting, but these bands remain non-chiral. Our results highlight the importance of relativistic effects even in $3d$ magnets with altermagnetic symmetries.

</details>


### [115] [Field-resolved hierarchy of superconducting energy gaps in PdTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12075)
*Sangyun Lee,A. M. Donald,D. Duong,S. Huang,R. Gazizulin,C. Huan,Rongying Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过低温软点接触谱测量，PdTe 的超导态表现出两个特征能隙（约0.5和1.1 meV），二者在升温时同时消失于同一Tc，但在磁场下却表现出不同的特征场（约4.5和7.5 kG），揭示了PdTe中存在具有不同磁场鲁棒性的超导能级层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清多个超导能标在磁场下的演化规律，对于区分多带、各向异性以及表面敏感的超导响应至关重要。作者旨在探究PdTe超导态中不同超导能标的磁场响应差异。

Method: 在密封的³He浸没环境中进行低温软点接触谱（soft point-contact spectroscopy）测量，获得Andreev反射谱，并以此结合磁场依赖的能隙分析及零偏压电导分析来提取超导能标。

Result: Andreev反射谱显示PdTe存在约0.5和1.1 meV两个超导能标，单一能隙BTK模型无法拟合；两者随升温向同一Tc逐渐抑制。但在磁场下，二者表现出两个不同的特征场（约4.5和7.5 kG）：低能标对应零电阻超导态的消失，而高能谱响应在高得多的磁场下仍持续存在。磁场依赖的能隙分析所得抑制标度与零偏压电导分析结果一致。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了PdTe中超导能标的磁场分辨层次结构，表明其超导响应具有不同的磁场鲁棒性，暗示存在多种来源的超导行为（如体态与表面态或多带贡献）。

Abstract: Resolving how multiple superconducting energy scales evolve under magnetic field is important for distinguishing multiband, anisotropic, and surface-sensitive superconducting responses. Here, we investigate the superconducting state of PdTe using low-temperature soft point-contact spectroscopy in a sealed $^3$He immersion environment. The resulting Andreev-reflection spectra reveal two characteristic superconducting energy scales near 0.5 and 1.1 meV that are not adequately described by a single-gap BTK model. Both energy scales are progressively suppressed upon warming toward the same superconducting transition temperature $T_c$. In contrast, their magnetic-field evolution reveals two distinct characteristic fields near 4.5 and 7.5 kG. The lower scale is associated with the loss of the zero-resistance superconducting state, whereas the higher-energy spectroscopic response persists to substantially larger fields. Independent field-dependent gap analysis yields suppression scales consistent with those identified from the zero-bias conductance. These results reveal a field-resolved hierarchy of superconducting energy scales in PdTe and indicate superconducting responses with distinct magnetic-field robustness.

</details>


### [116] [Strange-metal behavior in correlated molecular conductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12044)
*Savita Priya,Maxim Wenzel,Christian Prange,Bertold Rasche,Christian Sindlinger,Takuya Kobayashi,Hiromi Taniguchi,Dita Puspita Sari,Yasuyuki Ishii,Kazuya Miyagawa,Kazushi Kanoda,Simone Fratini,Martin Dressel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究κ-(BEDT-TTF)₄Hg₃₋δX₈(X=Br, Cl)盐类，发现其表现为"奇异金属"（strange metal）——电阻率ρ(T)∝T、频率依赖电导率|σ̂(ω)|∝ω^{-0.7}，散射率呈线性且达普朗克耗散尺度，并将该行为与不相称阴离子层及其大尺度空间调制直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: κ-BEDT-TTF盐是典型的Mott体系，通常实现从费米液体态到Mott绝缘体的关联驱动电子转变，但含空间调制Hg阴离子的金属化合物κ-(BEDT-TTF)₄Hg₃₋δX₈却不符合这一分类，其奇异金属行为的起源亟待阐明。

Method: 对κ-(BEDT-TTF)₄Hg₃₋δX₈(X=Br, Cl)进行温度依赖的输运测量和宽频段光学测量，分析电阻率随温度的变化规律、频率依赖电导率的幂律行为，以及散射率的频率演化与普朗克耗散的关系。

Result: 发现电阻率呈ρ(T)∝T直至高温，频率依赖电导率满足|σ̂(ω)|∝ω^{-0.7}；这些奇异金属的线性频率散射率标度到普朗克耗散。该响应仅出现在具有不相称阴离子层的体系中，并与大尺度空间调制直接关联。

Conclusion: 奇异金属行为源于不相称阴离子层引入的大尺度空间调制，该结论可推广到其他强关联奇异金属化合物，为理解奇异金属普适机制提供了新线索。

Abstract: The $κ$-BEDT-TTF salts are prototypical Mott systems, realizing a correlation-driven electronic transition from a Fermi-liquid state to a Mott insulator. The metallic compounds with spatially modulated Hg-containing anions, $κ$-(BEDT-TTF)$_4$Hg$_{3-δ}X_8$ ($X$ = Br, and Cl), however, fall outside this classification. Our temperature-dependent transport and broad-range optical investigations reveal a $ρ(T)\propto T$ dependence of the resistivity up to elevated temperatures and a power-law behavior of the frequency-dependent conductivity $\vert\hatσ (ω)\vert \propto ω^{-0.7}$; well-known hallmarks of ``strange metals''. The linear-in-frequency scattering rate of these strange metals scales to the Planckian dissipation. We demonstrate that this response only occurs in systems with incommensurate anion layers and establish a direct link to large-scale spatial modulations. Our conclusions can be extended to other strongly correlated strange-metal compounds

</details>


### [117] [Dynamical many-body control of high harmonic generation in a Mott insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12136)
*Mingyao Guo,Ryo Noguchi,Mohammad Muntasir Hassan,Steven J. Gomez Alvarado,Reina M. Gomez,Yao-Jui Chan,Yu-Chieh Wen,Stephen D. Wilson,Yao Wang,David Hsieh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了在典型Mott绝缘体La2CuO4中实现高达23阶的高次谐波产生，观测到与驱动场强无关的4 eV截止能量，揭示出由Hubbard带定义的固有能量尺度，并通过时间分辨高次谐波光谱学区分了载流子产生、带内加速与带间复合的贡献，证明光掺杂会触发多体流形的重构并抑制非线性带内电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高次谐波产生已成为探测固体中超快电子动力学的有力工具，但在强关联材料中，电子激发源于多体相互作用而非单粒子能带色散，该领域仍处于起步阶段，亟需厘清多体效应如何影响高次谐波发射及其作为关联电子重构探针的可行性。

Method: 以Mott绝缘体La2CuO4为研究对象，进行高次谐波产生实验（观测至23阶）；测量截止能量随驱动场强的依赖关系；采用时间分辨高次谐波光谱学（time-resolved high harmonic spectroscopy）分离载流子产生、带内加速与带间复合对非平衡辐射谱的不同贡献，并研究光掺杂（photo-doping）激发密度对多体流形重构的调控作用。

Result: 观测到La2CuO4中高达23阶的高次谐波；存在一个稳健的4 eV截止能量，且与驱动场强无关，对应Hubbard带所定义的固有能量尺度；发现光掺杂在超过临界激发密度后触发多体流形的基本重构，该过程重整化能带曲率并抑制非线性带内电流，从而将多体能带重构的印记直接写入谐波发射中。

Conclusion: 高次谐波产生可作为探测微妙关联驱动电子重构的灵敏探针；多体相互作用能够实现极端且高度可调的非线性光学响应，并可在非平衡态中加以调控，为强关联材料超快电子动力学研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: High harmonic generation (HHG) has emerged as a powerful probe of ultrafast electron dynamics in solids. However, in strongly correlated materials, where electronic excitations arise from many-body interactions rather than single-particle band dispersion, the field remains in its infancy. Here, we report HHG up to the 23$^{rd}$ order in the prototypical Mott insulator La$_2$CuO$_4$. We observe a robust 4 eV cutoff that is independent of driving field strength, revealing an intrinsic energy scale defined by the Hubbard bands. Using time-resolved high harmonic spectroscopy, we disentangle the distinct contributions of carrier creation, intraband acceleration, and interband recombination to the nonequilibrium radiation spectrum. We demonstrate that photo-doping triggers a fundamental reorganization of the many-body manifolds above a critical excitation density, a process that renormalizes the band curvature and suppresses nonlinear intraband currents, thereby directly imprinting the signature of many-body band reconstruction onto the harmonic emission. These results establish HHG as a sensitive probe of subtle correlation-driven electronic reconstructions and highlight that many-body interactions enable extreme nonlinear optical responses that are highly tunable in nonequilibrium states.

</details>


### [118] [Orbital magnetic susceptibility and de Haas-van Alphen effect of a flat band from quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12193)
*Ethan Huecker,Mengxing Ye,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 磁场在零场平带中诱导出有效色散，其一阶偏移由轨道矩 \mathcal{M}、二阶偏移由 \mathcal{X} 决定，二者与 Berry 曲率 \Omega 共同确定整个轨道响应；由此得到纯几何的磁化率、\mathcal{M}=0 轮廓形成的费米面及修正的 Lifshitz–Kosevich 振荡，并用数值对角化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零磁场下严格平坦的能带速度为零、有效质量发散、不存在费米面，因此描述轨道磁性的标准理论失效，需要建立适用于（近）平带的轨道响应理论。

Method: 利用非对易动量的 Moyal 代数对能带能量作展开，推导出磁场导致的一阶偏移 B\mathcal{M}(\mathbf k) 与二阶偏移 \tfrac12\mathcal{X}(\mathbf k)B^2，并给出 \mathcal{X} 的规范不变闭合表达式；结合 Berry 曲率 \Omega 构建完整轨道响应，对 \mathcal{M}=0 轮廓附近激发进行玻色化以获得修正的 Lifshitz–Kosevich 公式，并在扁平化的 Chern 晶格模型上做精确对角化验证。

Result: 磁化率具有纯几何形式，对一般孤立平带重现已有的规范不变响应公式；化学势钉扎于平带时，\mathcal{M}=0 轮廓成为涌现费米面，量子振荡为能级间距正比于 B^2 的修正 Lifshitz–Kosevich 形式，相移由绕涌现费米面的 Berry 相位与 \mathcal{X} 共同给出；当能带窄而不严格平坦时，振荡频率以无参数剖面从零场费米面面积渡越到 \mathcal{M}=0 轮廓面积，数值结果与分析一致。

Conclusion: 通过磁场诱导的有效色散，平带轨道磁性可由几何量（轨道矩、二阶系数 \mathcal{X} 与 Berry 曲率）完全刻画，并揭示 \mathcal{M}=0 轮廓作为涌现费米面的量子振荡及窄带下的频率渡越行为。

Abstract: A band that is exactly flat at zero magnetic field has zero velocity, a divergent effective mass, and no Fermi surface, so the standard theory of orbital magnetism does not apply. We show that the magnetic field generates an effective dispersion. In the expansion organized by the Moyal algebra of the noncommutative kinetic momenta, the band energy acquires a first-order shift $B\,\mathcal{M}(\mathbf k)$, set by the orbital moment of the Bloch state, and a second-order shift $\tfrac12\mathcal{X}(\mathbf k)B^{2}$, whose gauge-invariant coefficient we obtain in closed form. These two functions, together with the Berry curvature $Ω$, fix the entire orbital response. The susceptibility is purely geometric, and for a generic isolated flat band it reproduces the established gauge-invariant response formula. With the chemical potential pinned to the flat band, the $\mathcal{M}=0$ contour becomes an emergent Fermi surface, and bosonizing excitations in its vicinity yields a modified Lifshitz--Kosevich formula with level spacing quadratic in $B$. The phase shift of the oscillations is given jointly by the Berry phase around the emergent Fermi surface and $\mathcal{X}$. When the band is narrow rather than exactly flat, the oscillation frequency crosses over, with a parameter-free profile, from the zero-field Fermi-surface area to the area of the $\mathcal{M}=0$ contour. We verify our analytical findings numerically by exactly diagonalizing a flattened Chern lattice model.

</details>


### [119] [Transducing quantum-spin-ice correlations into Weyl Fermi-arc transport at a synthetic Kondo lattice interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12294)
*Tsung-Chi Wu,Michael Terilli,Christian Zaprianov,Eun Sang Choi,David Graf,Qinghua Zhang,Lin Gu,Mikhail Kareev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究在Weyl半金属Eu₂Ir₂O₇与量子自旋冰候选材料Tb₂Ti₂O₇之间构建了人工Kondo晶格界面，发现量子自旋冰关联会被"印刻"到Weyl费米弧输运中：超低温下电子输运随磁场从六重各向异性演变为十二重各向异性，再到高场复归六重，而经典自旋冰界面无此十二重响应，表明量子涨落的Tb矩通过与Weyl表面态耦合传递了横向多极自由度信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋冰的集体激发与量子涨落因材料绝缘而"电学沉默"，难以通过常规输运探测；将这一磁性环境与表面局域Weyl态耦合，可把其关联直接编码进费米弧输运，但该实验机制此前几乎未被探索。

Method: 研究者构建了Weyl半金属Eu₂Ir₂O₇与量子自旋冰候选材料Tb₂Ti₂O₇之间的合成Kondo晶格界面，并在超低温下测量界面电子输运的各向异性随外加磁场的变化；同时引入经典自旋冰界面作为对照实验。

Result: 在超低温下，电子输运在低场表现为六重各向异性，中等场窗口内演化为十二重各向异性，高场又复归六重各向异性。经典自旋冰界面未出现十二重响应。这一量子—经典对比表明，同一巡游电子通道在与量子涨落Tb矩相互作用时获得了定性不同的对称性信息。

Conclusion: 实验结果与界面耦合模型一致：Weyl表面态不仅耦合纵向偶极自由度，还耦合横向多极自由度。该工作确立了合成Kondo晶格界面作为一种新途径，可将带有多极关联的阻挫量子磁性转导为独特的Weyl费米弧输运响应。

Abstract: Quantum spin ice hosts collective excitations and quantum fluctuations that are largely electrically silent because the materials supporting them are insulating. Coupling this magnetic environment to surface-localized Weyl states offers a route to encode its correlations directly into Fermi-arc transport. Experimental access to this regime, however, has remained largely unexplored. Here, we realize a synthetic Kondo lattice interface between the Weyl semimetal $\mathrm{Eu_2Ir_2O_7}$ and the quantum-spin-ice candidate $\mathrm{Tb_2Ti_2O_7}$, and demonstrate that quantum-spin-ice correlations are imprinted on Weyl Fermi-arc transport. At ultra-low temperature, electronic transport evolves from a sixfold anisotropic response at low field into a twelvefold response over an intermediate field window, before entering a reentrant sixfold anisotropy at high field. No analogous twelvefold response occurs at the classical-spin-ice interface. The quantum--classical contrast shows that the same itinerant electronic channel acquires qualitatively different symmetry information when interacting with the quantum-fluctuating Tb moments, consistent with an interfacial coupling between the Weyl surface states and the transverse, multipolar degrees of freedom, in addition to the longitudinal, dipolar contribution. Our work establishes synthetic Kondo lattice interfaces as a route for transducing frustrated quantum magnetism with multipole correlations into distinct Weyl Fermi-arc transport responses.

</details>


### [120] [True vs false Fermi surfaces in the Pseudogap regime and their transformation with doping and temperature in the Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12339)
*Y. M. Vilk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用改进的双粒子自洽方法（TPSC+），本文同时分析能量分布曲线（EDC）与动量分布曲线（MDC），建立赝能隙区域中真/假费米面与零能量准粒子（ZEQ）线的分类，并揭示赝能隙由二维临界热自旋涨落驱动，基准测试表明 TPSC+ 在强相互作用区低估赝能隙抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的图解量子蒙特卡洛（DiagMC）对最近邻 Hubbard 模型的结果促使对赝能隙进行更细致的研究。传统费米液体术语在赝能隙区域失效，需要区分真、假费米面和零能量准粒子线，以准确刻画强关联电子体系中的赝能隙物理。

Method: 采用改进的双粒子自洽方法（TPSC+），同时分析能量分布曲线（EDC）和动量分布曲线（MDC）；通过定义假费米面（动量空间谱有极大值但在零能量频率空间有凹陷）和假零能量准粒子线（违反标准准粒子条件 ∂Σ'(k,ω)/∂ω|_{ω=0}<0）进行分类；并以 Matsubara 谱代理 -Im[G(k,iπT)]/π 与 DiagMC 结果进行基准对比，同时使用 A(k,0) 分析费米面的演化。

Result: 赝能隙由临界热自旋涨落驱动，该涨落因 Mermin-Wagner 定理而出现在二维体系中。共度涨落首先打开反节点赝能隙，留下真费米弧；随温度降低，费米面演化为空穴型和电子型假费米面。非共度涨落在对角线附近产生热点，赝能隙持续到量子临界点（QCP），而在 k_AN 处赝能隙在 QCP 掺杂之前消失。在 k_AN 处谱中出现两条前驱反铁磁（AFM）能带，均处于未占据（ω>0）区域。TPSC+ 在强相互作用区低估赝能隙抑制，需在更低温度或掺杂下才重现 DiagMC 行为；谱代理等效于热展宽 η=πT 的谱函数，当 πT 不是最小能量尺度时会掩盖特征。利用 A(k,0) 发现空穴型费米面在任意相互作用强度、低温下均出现，甚至在弱耦合下也存在。

Conclusion: 费米液体语言在赝能隙区域失效，必须区分真/假费米面与零能量准粒子线；赝能隙的本质由二维临界热自旋涨落（共度与非共度）驱动，导致费米弧、真假费米面演化及前驱 AFM 能带等丰富现象。TPSC+ 方法在强耦合区存在系统低估，且 Matsubara 谱代理因热展宽可能掩盖真实谱特征，强调直接使用 A(k,0) 分析的重要性。

Abstract: Exact diagrammatic quantum Monte Carlo (DiagMC) results for the nearest-neighbor Hubbard model motivate a closer study of the pseudogap. Using the improved two-particle self-consistent approach (TPSC+), we analyze Energy (EDC) and Momentum Distribution Curves (MDC) simultaneously. We show that Fermi-liquid terminology breaks down in the pseudogap regime, requiring a distinction between true and false Fermi surfaces and zero-energy quasiparticle (ZEQ) lines. A false Fermi surface has a momentum-space spectral maximum but a frequency-space depression at zero energy, while a false ZEQ line violates the standard quasiparticle condition $\partial Σ'(\mathbf{k},ω)/\partial ω|_{ω=0}<0$.The pseudogap is driven by critical thermal spin fluctuations, which occur in two dimensions because of the Mermin-Wagner theorem. Commensurate fluctuations first open an antinodal pseudogap, leaving true Fermi arcs. With decreasing temperature, the Fermi surface evolves into hole- and electron-like false Fermi surfaces. Incommensurate fluctuations generate hot spots near the diagonal, where the pseudogap persists to the quantum critical point (QCP), whereas at $\mathbf{k}_{AN}$ it disappears before the QCP doping. There, the spectrum has two precursor antiferromagnetic (AFM) bands, both in the unoccupied ($ω>0$) region. We benchmark TPSC+ against DiagMC results for the Matsubara spectral proxy $-\mathrm{Im}[\mathcal{G}(\mathbf{k},iπT)]/π$. TPSC+ underestimates pseudogap suppression in the strong-interaction regime, reproducing DiagMC behavior at lower temperatures or doping. This proxy is equivalent to the spectral function with thermal broadening $η=πT$, which obscures features when $πT$ is not the smallest energy scale. Using $A(\mathbf{k},0)$, we find that hole-like Fermi surfaces emerge at any interaction strength at low temperature, even at weak coupling.

</details>
